238. Hub failover (wireweave a50173f), local relay, distinct tones per peer, scripts/ship/voice-room.mjs. Causes: each peer elected itself hub (25% hysteresis) so three peers ended with three hubs; hub loss was noticed only on closed or failed state (about 10 s); offers during hub switches were dropped; _renegotiate was defined twice with opposite argument order so camera and screen share renegotiation was silently dropped. Fixes: election by agreement (presence carries hub claim and uplinkClass, majority then class then pubkey, joiner adopts the existing hub), hub heartbeat 1 s with 4 s silence as loss, stale peers pruned, offers accepted from known participants and re-sent up to 3 times, duplicate removed, video relayed like audio. Measured (ms, new hub agreed / all remaining hear each other / late joiner hears): close 3 peers 3727/4982/1315, close 4 peers 4572/7212/1430, kill 3 4062/5336/1304, kill 4 5088/6370/1285, frozen plus offline 3 4253/6774/1305, frozen 4 4084/5388/1318. Before: 9.9 to 11 s, once a 22 s split. Thread lanes survive hub loss (the dead hub's lane never shrank and later played). One frozen 4-peer run failed under CPU load (22.5 s) and passed alone 4.1 s; cause under starvation not isolated. Not aligned: every voiced word of the dead hub after failover.

239. Plain voice room, three peers (room3 11 of 11, video3 8 of 8): own tone per speaker, no swap, mute inaudible, deafen hears nothing and others unaffected, hub leaving gives a new hub in 26 ms and all hear each other after 2477 ms, rejoin 1221 ms, camera from the hub and screen share from a non-hub reach everyone (640x360, 1440x810). Not exercised: second failure in a row, TURN/NAT, real hardware.

240. Residual loss, idle host (load under 3). Two peers, 30 runs at 1x, missing voiced ms net of baseline: set 1 all 0, set 2 one 40, set 3 one 40; 30 of 30 at or under 40 ms. Metric: 10 ms frames, validated on planted 100, 60 and 40 ms gaps, outer 100 ms of the compared span ignored (source end artefact), floor [12570,12600] from the baseline run. Fixes: splice pairs the clip with the hole whose length best matches its muted window (the old code filled the latest hole: 440 to 700 ms losses), wider splice margins (5 frames measured, 10 on the 80 ms fallback). Three peers, 10 runs, hub forced via a getStats wrapper in the witness, 6 with the hub interrupting, all peers agreed on the hub every run: longest single gap 80 ms, but each speaker has 1 to 7 scattered 30 to 70 ms gaps, per-speaker totals A 0,40,40,50,40,140,150,160,40,50 and B 90,80,200,210,350,220,120,330,140,190. If the bar is no single gap over 100 ms it holds; if it is total loss over 100 ms per speaker it does NOT (up to 350 ms). Cause of the scatter not proven (no correlation with clip count, absent in the no-interruption baseline). Documented as a known limit in AGENTS.md. No worklet-timestamp trace done.

241. Scale to five peers, per-peer upload over a 20 s talk, everyone talking, kbps rtp payload and transport: mesh each peer 2:48.4/69.2, 3:96.8/138.4, 4:145.2/207.5, 5:193.6/276.7; hub topology hub 2:48.4/69.7, 3:161.6/234.9, 4:339.7/495.6, 5:564.3/1027.3 and 562.7/829.8, non-hub 48.4/70 constant. Hub rtp = 48.4(N-1) + 32.4(N-1)(N-2). A 1 Mbps uplink saturates at 5 peers all talking (0.83 to 1.03 Mbps measured). Extrapolated, not run: 6 peers 1.30 Mbps, 7 1.85, 8 2.49. The old 4508d66 code was already hub elected; the mesh column is the current code with election and relay disabled. The voice settings modal has a How voice rooms connect row with these numbers (design 11bc469 successor).

242. Hub failover hardening (wireweave pin in this ship). Second consecutive failure (failover2 modes, 3 and 4 peers, close, kill, frozen plus offline): re-election and every remaining peer heard every other at the audio elements; idle new hub agreed 4.9 to 5.3 s, all hear 6.1 to 6.3 s (one closed 4-peer outlier 10.0 s, unexplained); with 3 peers the second failure leaves one peer connected, own hub, no page error, and a late joiner hears and is heard. Found and fixed an older bug: the hub's relay signal used one replaceable d tag for every source, so signals to a peer replaced each other (crash and offline 4-peer runs left a peer unheard for 15 to 36 s, reproduced on a50173f 3 of 3); the d tag now carries the stream id and the 1 s heartbeat repeats the relay map; failover:crash:4 then heard everyone in 6.2 to 7.5 s in 7 of 7 runs. Liveness (src/hub-liveness.js): a hub is alive while either the data channel heartbeat or the connection transport bytesReceived moves; silence budget max(4 s, 3 times the recent largest evidence gap) capped at 8 s; a late watchdog tick (over 2.5 s) skips the check for 1.5 s. A 9 s busy loop on a live hub made the old code fail (no recovery in 31 s) and the new code pass; busy peer passes. Frozen hub under a synthetic hog (8 spinners on 4 cores, load 3.3 to 16.3, stopped by PID), three runs in a row: 3 peers agree 5027, 5047, 5048 ms, all hear 6308, 6301, 6318, late joiner 1309, 1283, 1249; 4 peers agree 5025, 5147, 5195, all hear 7629, 7739, 6525, late 1302, 1273, 1304. No-hog single failure agree 5.0 to 5.3 s, all hear 6.2 to 7.5 s (slower than the 3.7 to 5.1 s of entry 238, the price of the transport evidence). Weak spot: a late joiner after a failure takes 3.6 to 15.6 s when dead peers' presence is still inside the 15 s stale window (route timeout 10 s unchanged). Not covered: a hub that is frozen yet keeps sending ICE consent traffic, real networks, TURN and NAT, real hardware.

243. Close-out, owner reports 2026-11-22 to 2026-11-25. Fully evidenced by live runs on the local relay at 1440x900 and 390x844 (entries 228 to 242): server isolation (ISOLATION_OK, gated), redraw storm (REDRAW_OK, 200 of 200 clicks, idle 0, gated, live sidebar confirmed by the dispatcher), cancel and close contract (sweep 398 rows, gated), discovery, profile setup, account panes, icons, split select, profile card and layout, delete server as a tombstone (TOMBSTONE_OK), deploy guard, hub failover and voice room regression (voice-room.mjs, opt-in). Bounded: real hardware (iOS and Android safe area, keyboard, notch, fling, long-press, swipe, audible audio, device latency; mobile is emulated touch only), TURN and NAT (all voice witnesses are localhost, no relayed candidates), six or more peers (measured to five, rest extrapolated; hub saturates 1 Mbps at five all talking), public relays (NIP-09 support is documented from NIP-11 for six relays, no deletion was witnessed on any; four relays did not answer). Accepted as best effort with a known limit: three-peer voice thread loss (single gaps at most 80 ms, per-speaker totals up to 350 ms, cause unproven). Cosmetic observations left: split select label clips at 120 px (game-3 caps at 96 px), Shared sessions rail style, Recap What I Missed in the server menu.

244. Late joiner tombstone and the 10.0 s outlier: NOT shipped, work reverted from the checkouts and kept as patches in scratchpad/unshipped-hub3 (wireweave.patch 281 lines, zellous.patch 193 lines). Findings: the 10.0 s closed 4-peer outlier (failover2:close:4, entry 242) is a re-election of an already dead peer: the dead hub still sat in the 15 s presence window and in the claims of peers that had not noticed yet, a peer that had declared it lost without a tombstone was voted back to it by the majority claim and then waited out HUB_ROUTE_TIMEOUT_MS (10 s); reproduced in an offline 3-peer joiner run (hub lost 1.4 s, dead hub re-elected 2.4 s, lost again 9.4 s). The patch buries a declared-dead peer (local participants, election, a dead list with its last presence ts carried by presence and the hub heartbeat, cleared by newer presence), lowers the silence floor to 2.5 s, and fixes ghost relays of the dead hub; with it agreement fell to about 3.1 to 3.5 s and 20 runs of failover2:close:4 had second agree 2.8 to 3.7 s in the first batch. Why it was not shipped: on the final code the late-joiner and rejoin bar failed more than before the change: of 20 failover2:close:4 runs 7 late joiners took 7.6 to 9.0 s and two second agreements took 8.0 s; single failovers had a late joiner never heard (31.2 s) and 9.1 s; same-pubkey rejoin took 7.8 to 31 s in 3 of 4 runs; offline 4-peer late join 2 of 5 over 3 s (3285, 4136); the hog run had a late joiner never heard (30 s) and one at 7.9 s. Tried and reverted: connect timeout 5 s (room3 failed 3 of 5), DTLS fingerprint replacement (spurious tombstones). Shipped state stays wireweave 5d45124 (entry 242 numbers: agree 5.0 to 5.3 s, late joiner 1.3 s except when dead presence is inside the 15 s stale window, 3.6 to 15.6 s, and the 10.0 s outlier is explained above but not fixed). Open: rejoin and late joiner stall root cause (the rejoiner's connection to the hub never completes), hog 3-peer cells, the host was not idle during the final runs.

245. Late joiner and rejoin root cause (isolated on the shipped pin 5d45124, instrumented dbg event stream, idle host). Causes: A joiner elects the dead hub (its own unconfirmed claim plus the dead peer's replayed self-claim outvote the survivors; it waits as non-offerer, the only exit is the 8 to 10 s route timeout), 40 of 40 shipped late-join runs; B joiner prunes the live connection a new hub just offered, 13 of 20 at 3 peers and 1 of 20 at 4; Z the other side keeps a zombie connection after a one-sided close and never re-offers, same 13 runs; F _attachRelayed reuses the relayed entry without updating via so the later close of the old hub drops the new hub's audio, 5 of 20 at 4 peers; E onended of a closed connection calls _doIceRestart on the old peer object which 8 s later closes the new connection to the same pubkey, 2 of 6 shipped 4-peer rejoins (30 s); G a rejoiner elects itself hub from claims naming its previous life; R a rejoin before survivors detect the old death is suspected until its next heartbeat; ICE the first pc of a late joiner emits no candidate for 3 s or more, about 1 in 8 offline-scenario joins in both versions; T a dead peer's replayed self-claim ties the lone live hub and the class tie-break picks the dead one until its presence passes 15 s. Shipped outcome at 3 peers: heard in 2 of 20, 10.6 to 11.4 s in 5, never within 22 s in 13; at 4 peers heard in 14 of 20, never in 6. Fix (unshipped, patch in scratchpad/hub4/final): latched own claim, claims void before the claimed hub's join presence, reborn close, 10 s presence lapse loses ties, prune only once connected and never a self-claiming hub, bye signal with DTLS fingerprint, stale ICE restart ignored, via updated, 3 s gather-stall recreate. Fixed outcome: late joiner at 2 s, 3 peers 3390 to 4077 ms after join (10 of 10, shipped 7 never), 4 peers 3622 to 4322 (10 of 10, shipped 2 never); at 12 s 3 peers 1268 to 1803, 4 peers 1215 to 1750 (shipped one never); same-pubkey rejoin 3 peers 1249 to 1280, 4 peers 1257 to 1349 (shipped 2501 to 3793 and 2565 to 2655, 2 of 6 stuck 30 s earlier). Regression on the fixed code: room3 11 of 11, video3 8 of 8, 12 failover and failover2 cells pass, hog 4-peer no 36 s non-recovery, failover2:close:4 20 runs second agree max 5268 (shipped 8356) and late joiner max 2624 (shipped 16913). NOT SHIPPED because the rule was every number beats 5d45124 and these do not: failover2:close:4 first agreement median +21 ms and max +44 ms; 4-peer hog hear median +189 ms in one batch (minus 302 ms in another); 3-peer hog late joiner max 2340 versus 644 ms (two runs); single failure killed 4 late joiner max 1824 versus 676 ms among shipped runs that completed (shipped also had 30 s non-recoveries); closed 4 hear max +63 ms; frozen 4 agree median +22 ms; late joiner at 12 s with 3 peers max 1803 versus 1774. Unfixed in both versions: a second failure where the new hub freezes before anyone connected to it gives an 8 s second agreement (3 of 5 shipped, 4 of 10 fixed). Not witnessed: real networks, TURN and force-relay, clock skew between peers' presence timestamps, hub close and offline in the late-join cells (crash by PID only). Harness incidents: the host reached 100 percent disk once (21 GB of chromium profiles, cleaned); campaigns sometimes started at load 4 to 5; two pkill -f of its own scripts against the by-PID rule.

246. Late joiner and rejoin fix shipped (wireweave pin in this ship), dispatcher decision 2026-11-27: the rule is no regression of substance, not every digit. Fix and numbers are in entry 245. Tail numbers that do not beat 5d45124: failover2:close:4 first agreement median +21 ms and max +44 ms (5047 vs 5026, max 5393 vs 5349); 4-peer hog hear median +189 ms in one batch and minus 302 ms in another; 3-peer hog late joiner max 2340 versus 644 ms (two runs); killed 4-peer late joiner max 1824 versus 676 ms among the runs shipped completed (shipped also had two 30 s non-recoveries and two 15 s late joiners in those cells); closed 4-peer hear max +63 ms; frozen 4-peer agree median +22 ms; 12 s late joiner with 3 peers max 1803 versus 1774 ms. Fixed what shipped failed: 13 of 20 never-heard late joiners at 3 peers and 6 of 20 at 4, the 30 s same-pubkey stalls, the 16.9 s late joiner in failover2:close:4. Unfixed in both versions: a second failure where the new hub freezes before anyone connected to it gives an 8 s second agreement (3 of 5 shipped runs, 4 of 10 fixed runs), no heartbeat or byte evidence exists yet. Unwitnessed: real networks, TURN and force-relay (the gather-stall watch is skipped there), clock skew between peers (claim epochs compare presence timestamps from different clocks), the 3 s gather-stall threshold on slow networks, and hub close or offline in the late-join and rejoin cells (crash by PID only).
